to obtain the result that:

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\geq C\left(\frac{12}{\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2d}{n\sqrt{h}}+\frac{10\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{nh}}+\frac{6\sqrt{d}}{d}+\frac{5\sqrt{n}}{n}+\frac{\sqrt{d}}{n}\right)\right)\leq 2e^{-cd}+2e^{-ch}+6e^{-cn}.

Part of (ix).

Here we try to give the lower bound of
\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}\right\rVert
, since
{\bm{A}}_{2}
is rank-1 matrix, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right\rVert^{2}=\frac{1}{n^{2}h}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h}tr({\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h}tr({\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h}\left\lVert{\bm{a}}\right\rVert^{2}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{n^{2}h}\left\lVert{\bm{a}}\right\rVert^{2}\left({\bm{\xi}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}+2{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right).

Following
(
3
)
, we define events
{\bm{E}}_{2}
,
{\bm{E}}_{4}
and
{\bm{E}}_{5}
by

\displaystyle{\bm{E}}_{2}:=\left\{\left\lVert{\bm{X}}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{d}+2\sqrt{n}\right\},

\displaystyle{\bm{E}}_{4}:=\left\{\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq 2+\frac{h}{d}\right\},

\displaystyle{\bm{E}}_{5}:=\left\{\left|tr({\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top})-nh\right|\leq\frac{nh}{2}\right\}.

Based on (

) and (

), we know that

\mathbb{P}({\bm{E}}_{2})\geq 1-2e^{-cn},\quad\mathbb{P}({\bm{E}}_{4})\geq 1-2e^{-cd}.

We also know that by sub-gaussian and sub-exponential concentration inequality

\mathbb{P}(\left|{\bm{W}}_{1_{i,j}}^{0}\right|\leq 1)\geq 1-2e^{-cd},\quad\mathbb{P}(\left|{{\bm{W}}_{1_{i,j}}^{0}}^{2}-1\right|\leq 1)\geq 1-2e^{-c\sqrt{d}}.

(48)

Based on Lemma
C.3
and (
48
), we have

\mathbb{P}({\bm{E}}_{5})\geq 1-2e^{-cn}-2nhde^{-cd}-2nh(d^{2}-d)e^{-c\sqrt{d}}\geq 1-2e^{-cn}-4nhd^{2}e^{-c\sqrt{d}}

.

Thus condition on
{\bm{E}}_{2}\cap{\bm{E}}_{4}
, it is easy to find that
\mathbb{E}\left\lVert{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\leq C_{4}n(n^{2}+h^{2})
, also based on (
38
), by Lemma

, we have

\mathbb{P}\left({\bm{\xi}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\leq\frac{\rho_{e}^{2}}{2}nh-t|{\bm{E}}_{2}\cap{\bm{E}}_{5}\right)\leq 2e^{-c\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{n(n^{2}+h^{2})},\frac{t}{n+h}\right\}}.

Choosing
t=\frac{\rho_{e}^{2}}{4}nd
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left({\bm{\xi}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\leq\frac{\rho_{e}^{2}}{8}nh\right)\leq 2e^{-c\min\left\{\frac{nh^{2}}{(n^{2}+h^{2})},\frac{nh}{n+h}\right\}}+4e^{-cn}+4nhd^{2}e^{-c\sqrt{d}}.

(49)

In addition, by Lemma
C.2
, condition on
{\bm{E}}_{2}
and
{\bm{E}}_{4}
, we know that

\left\lVert{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right\rVert_{\psi_{2}}\leq C_{5}\frac{\sqrt{n(n^{2}+d^{2})(h^{2}+d^{2})}}{d},

one can easily see that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right|\geq t|{\bm{E}}_{2}\cap{\bm{E}}_{4}\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{cd^{2}t^{2}}{n(n^{2}+d^{2})(h^{2}+d^{2})}}.

Thus, let
t=\frac{\rho_{e}^{2}}{32}nd
, we obtain

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right|\geq\frac{\rho_{e}^{2}}{32}nd\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{cnd^{4}}{(n^{2}+d^{2})(h^{2}+d^{2})}}+2e^{-cn}+2e^{-cd}.

(50)

Based on (
41
),(

)and(
50
) , we arrive at

\displaystyle 6\left(e^{-c\min\left\{\frac{nd^{2}}{(n^{2}+d^{2})},\frac{nd}{n+d},\frac{nd^{4}}{(n^{2}+d^{2})(h^{2}+d^{2})}\right\}}+e^{-cn}+e^{-ch}\right)

\displaystyle+6\left(e^{-cd}+nhd^{2}e^{-c\sqrt{d}}\right)

Part of (x).

),(

) and (
C.1.3

, we arrive at

\displaystyle\leq 8e^{-cd}+6e^{-ch}.

Proposition C.8
.

(Three-layer NN setting under Gaussian initialization.) Under Assumption
E.1
, there exists some constant
c^{*}>0
such that for all large
n,h,d
with probability at least
1-32e^{-c^{*}n}-30n^{4}e^{-c^{*}\sqrt{n}}
, we have gradient approximation,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert,

(51)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert.

We obtain the norm control of gradient matrices,

\displaystyle\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\hskip 10.0pt\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),

(52)

\displaystyle\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\hskip 10.0pt\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle Small\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

(53)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

(54)

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

(55)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

(56)

Proof of Proposition
E.2
.

By Lemma
C.6
, We know that in the proportional regime, there exist constants
C^{*},c^{*}>0
such that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq C^{*}\frac{1}{n}\right)\geq 1-14e^{-c^{*}n}.

On the other hand, part(iv) in Lemma
C.6
implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\geq C^{*}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\geq 1-18e^{-c^{*}n}.

Conditioning on the two events stated above, we have

\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert).

We finally obtain that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\right)\geq 1-34e^{-c^{*}n}

Similarly, We know that in the proportional regime, there exist constants
C^{*},c^{*}>0
such that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq C^{*}\frac{1}{n}\right)\geq 1-14e^{-c^{*}n}.

On the other hand, part(iv) in Lemma
C.6
implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\geq C^{*}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\geq 1-30n^{4}e^{-c^{*}\sqrt{n}}.

Conditioning on the two events stated above, we have

\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert).

We finally obtain that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\right)\geq 1-14e^{-c^{*}n}-30n^{4}e^{-c^{*}\sqrt{n}}.

Also we can get
\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1)
.

Since we have
\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(\sqrt{h}),\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(1),\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{d,\mathbb{P}}(\sqrt{h})
, based on Assumption
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle Small\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

\square

C.1.4
Two-layer Neural Network Case Under Gaussian Initialization

The one-step update equations for the two-layer neural network are as follows:

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}

(57)

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{2}{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}

where
W_{1}^{0},W_{2}^{0}
are the initial hidden layer weights,
W_{1}^{1},W_{2}^{1}
are the updated layer weights,
G_{1}^{0}
and
G_{2}^{0}
are the corresponding gradient matrix, where

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}}_{{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}}_{{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}}

(58)

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}}_{{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}}

(59)

Proposition C.9
.

(Two-layer neural network under gaussian initialization.) There exists some constants
p^{*},q^{*}>0
such that for all large
n,h,d
with high probability at least
1-p^{*}e^{-q^{*}n}

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

(60)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert.

(61)

There exists some constants
p,q>0
such that for all large
n,h,d
with high probability at least
1-pe^{-qn}

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

(62)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}.

(63)

Proof of Proposition
C.9
.

The proof is same to proofs of Proposition
E.2
and Lemma
C.6
.

C.2
Norm Analysis of Two-step Update Gradient Matrices

C.2.1
Two-layer Neural Network under Orthogonal Initialization

Proposition C.10
.

(Two-layer NN setting under Orthogonal initialization.) Under Assumption
3.2
, if
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\leq O(h\sqrt{h})
, we have the following gradient approximation,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert,

(64)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert.

And we have

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}

(65)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}

(66)

Proof of Proposition
C.10
.

Note that
{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}^{\top}
,
{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}
. Based on Lemma
C.5
, it is easy to get

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert,\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

We also have
{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}^{\top}
,
{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}^{\top}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}
. Thus we can obtain that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}

Since
{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}
,
{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}({\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}+\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}({\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}+\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert).

Thus, we get that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert,

(67)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert.

Based on this, we can get
\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}
.

Since we have
\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}
, based on Assumption
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}

(68)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}

(69)

\square

C.2.2
Three-layer Neural Network under Orthogonal Initialization

Proposition C.11
.

(Three-layer NN setting under Orthogonal initialization.) Under Assumption
3.2
, if
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\leq O(h)
, we have the following gradient approximation,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert,

(70)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert.

And we have

\displaystyle Small\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

(71)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

(72)

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}

(73)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}

(74)

Proof of Proposition
C.11
.

Note that
{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}^{\top}
,
{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}
. Based on Lemma
C.5
, it is easy to get

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert,\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

We also have
{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}^{\top}
,
{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}^{\top}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}}\widetilde{{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}
. Thus we can obtain that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}

Since
{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}
,
{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}({\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}+\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}({\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}+\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert).

Thus, we get that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert,

(75)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}-1}\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert.

Based on this, we can get
\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{G}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert_{F}
.

Since we have
\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F},\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert,\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}
, based on Assumption
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle Small\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert

(76)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

(77)

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}

(78)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}

(79)

\square

Appendix D
Orthogonal initialization

Here we give one-step and two-step test loss under whiten initialization, also we give the gap bound between the exact test loss and the approximate test loss.

Lemma D.1
.

Consider two stochastic random orthogonal matrices
{\bm{O}}_{1}
,
{\bm{O}}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{h\times h}
uniformly distributed on the orthogonal group with respect to the Haar measure (i.e.
{\bm{O}}_{1}{\bm{O}}_{1}^{\top}={\bm{O}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{O}}_{1}={\bm{I}}
,
{\bm{O}}_{2}{\bm{O}}_{2}^{\top}={\bm{O}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{O}}_{2}={\bm{I}}
), we have
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{O}}_{1}]=\mathbb{E}[{\bm{O}}_{2}]={\mathbf{0}},\mathbb{E}[{\bm{O}}_{1}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[{\bm{O}}_{2}^{2}]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}},\mathbb{E}[{\bm{O}}_{1}{\bm{O}}_{2}]={\mathbf{0}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.1
.

For orthogonal group, one key invariance property is for any fixed
{\bm{R}}
in orthogonal group, the distribution of
{\bm{Q}}{\bm{R}}
and
{\bm{R}}{\bm{Q}}
is same as
{\bm{Q}}
. In particular, note that
-{\bm{I}}
is in orthogonal group, therefore, we have

{\bm{Q}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{=}}(-{\bm{I}}){\bm{Q}}=-{\bm{Q}},

so based on this, we take a look at the expectation:
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[-{\bm{Q}}\right]=-\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}\right]
, which means we can get
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{1}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{2}\right]={\mathbf{0}}
. Also consider each row row (or column) of
{\bm{Q}}
is a random vector uniformly distributed on the unit sphere in
\mathbb{R}^{h}
. Hence by the definitions of orthogonal group, we have

\sum_{a=1}^{h}{\bm{Q}}_{ia}^{2}=1\;\Rightarrow\;\mathbb{E}\big[{\bm{Q}}_{ia}^{2}\big]=\frac{1}{h},

furthermore, if we consider flipping the sign of one row or one column like left-multiplying by
D=diag(-1,1,1,\cdots,1)
in orthogonal group, which flips the sign of every entry in the first row, but the distribution is unchanged. Thus, the expectation of any product involving an odd number of the entries from that row must be zero. Similarly for the flipping any column, so we can get unless
i=j
and
a=b
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{ia}{\bm{Q}}_{jb}\right]=0
, so it is easy to get
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{1}^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{2}^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
. Based on above arguments we can deduce that
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{Q}}_{1}{\bm{Q}}_{2}\right]={\mathbf{0}}
.
\square

D.1
Approximate one-step loss under orthogonal initialization for two-layer NN

Theorem D.2
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
3.2
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h\sqrt{h}})
, based on Proposition
5.1
and Lemma
5.2
, consider the training procedure discussed in Section
3
, we obtain the following test loss after one-step and two-step GD update in a two-layer neural network under orthogonal initialization:

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}

(80)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}-\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}+1

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}})=\frac{1}{h}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{4}+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{5}}-1\right)^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}

We prove Theorem
5.3
above by the following two subsection
D.1
and
D.2
.

For orthogonal initialization we follow assumption
3.2
that
n=h=d
.

Here we consider the whiten initialization which make the setting
{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}=h{\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}{\bm{M}}={\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
.

We consider a test data
\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}
under two-layer setting, where
\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}
is an random orthogonal vector, we have

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

(81)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right).

Here we define
L_{1},L_{2},L_{3},L_{4}
, where

\displaystyle L_{1}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{2}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{3}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{4}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

Thus

L_{\text{two-layer}}=L_{1}-L_{2}-L_{3}+L_{4}

We have
L_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{16}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}\right]\right)=\frac{1}{h},

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{4}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{6}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}},

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{4}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{6}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}},

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{6}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}},

\displaystyle T_{8}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h^{8}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{9}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{10}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{6}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}},

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{6}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}},

\displaystyle T_{12}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{13}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{6}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}},

\displaystyle T_{14}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h^{8}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{15}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{16}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{10}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}.

We have
L_{2}=\sum_{i=17}^{20}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{17}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{18}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{3}}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{19}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{20}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)=0.

We have
L_{3}=\sum_{i=21}^{24}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{21}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{22}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{3}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{23}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{24}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}\right]\right)=0,

Based on the above computation, we see that for orthogonal initialization, the one-step test loss for 2-layer NN is

L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}-\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}+1

(82)

Here, for the one-step updated loss, we consider the following optimization problem, and we assume the following constraint
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, our goal is to see whether
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is local minima or local maxima.

L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}-4h^{\alpha-2}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}+1

(83)

It is easy to find that
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is a local maxima.
\square

D.2
Approximate two-step loss for two-layer NN under orthogonal initialization

Here we consider the orthogonal initialization which make the setting
{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}=h{\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}{\bm{M}}={\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
.

For the simplification, we only consider replacing
{\bm{G}}_{1}
with
{\bm{A}}_{1}
and
{\bm{G}}_{2}
with
{\bm{A}}_{2}
.

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}

\displaystyle\;{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\;{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\;{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle\;{\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}

Thus we have

\displaystyle{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}

\displaystyle{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}

\displaystyle={\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}+\eta_{1}{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}{\bm{M}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}

\displaystyle={\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}+\eta_{2}{\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}}={\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{M}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

we can derive that

\displaystyle{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}

\displaystyle=(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{4}}{\bm{M}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{M}}

Consider the following loss

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right).

We first compute
tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}\right]\right)
, we find that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}=\frac{1}{h^{2}}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{4}{\bm{I}}_{h}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{6}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{6}}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})^{2}(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})^{2}}{h^{11}}{\bm{I}}_{h}+\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{7}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}{\bm{I}}_{h}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}+\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{9}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{4}+\frac{4(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})^{2}(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})^{2}}{h^{10}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}

Following the similar way, we get that

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}^{\top}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{5}},

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{5}},

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=1

We have

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{4}+\frac{4(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})^{2}(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})^{2}}{h^{10}}+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}-\frac{4(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{5}}+1

L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\frac{1}{h}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{4}+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}+\left(\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+h^{3})}{h^{5}}-1\right)^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{8\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}

(84)

\square

Corollary D.3
.

Suppose
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
and we consider
0<\alpha\leq\tfrac{3}{2}
.
Then, for any
\alpha
in this range, the point
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is not a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})
.
Moreover, for
1<\alpha\leq\tfrac{3}{2}
, if
h>\max\{{h^{*}},256\}
, then
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}})
, where
{h^{*}}
is the root of the following equation:

(1+o(1))h^{1-\alpha}+16h^{\alpha-2}+2h^{-\alpha}+8h^{\alpha-3}+6h^{3\alpha-6}-2=0

(85)

Proof of Corollary
5.4
.

Here, for the two-step updated loss, we consider the following optimization problem, and we assume that
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, we want to find whether the local minima for
L_{\text{two-layer}}
is
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
.

Since
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},,\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{3}})^{4}+\frac{16\eta_{1}^{2}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{2}}{h^{7}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{4(\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})+h^{3})}{h^{5-\alpha}}-1\right)^{2}+(1+\frac{\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{3}})^{2}\frac{8\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{5}}

Taking the derivative, we have

\displaystyle L^{\prime}_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=2(h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})\left[\frac{4}{h^{4}}(1+\frac{\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{3}})^{3}+\frac{32\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{7}}\right]

\displaystyle+2(h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})\left[\frac{8}{h^{5-\alpha}}\left(\frac{4(\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})+h^{3})}{h^{5-\alpha}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle+2(h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})\left[\frac{8}{h^{5}}+\frac{32\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{8}}+\frac{24\eta_{1}^{2}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{2}}{h^{11}}\right]

If we let
L_{\text{two-layer}}
is
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
be local minima, we must need

•

4>5-\alpha\Rightarrow\alpha>1

•

3(2\alpha-3)-4<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<\frac{8}{5}

•

2(2\alpha-3)-4<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<\frac{5}{3}

•

2\alpha-7<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<2

•

4\alpha-10<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<\frac{5}{3}

•

2\alpha-8<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<3

•

4\alpha-11<\alpha-5\Rightarrow\alpha<2

Take the intersection, we have
1<\alpha<\frac{8}{5}
. Given the fixed
1<\alpha<\frac{8}{5}
, we will give how large
h
is to ensure that
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
will are local minima,

Case 1.

If
\alpha=\frac{3}{2}
, we need

8h^{\frac{1}{2}}-32-32-64-o(1)>0,

which means
h>256+o(1)
.

Case 2.

If
1<\alpha<\frac{3}{2}
, we find
6\alpha-13<4\alpha-10<-4
and
1-\alpha>\alpha-2
, so we need

(4+o(1))h^{1-\alpha}+32h^{\alpha-2}+(32+o(1))h^{\alpha-2}+8h^{-\alpha}+32h^{\alpha-3}+24h^{3\alpha-6}-8<0,

which means

(1+o(1))h^{1-\alpha}+16h^{\alpha-2}+2h^{-\alpha}+8h^{\alpha-3}+6h^{3\alpha-6}-2<0,

Case 3.

If
\frac{3}{2}<\alpha<\frac{8}{5}
, we find
6\alpha-13>4\alpha-10>-4
, so we need

(4+o(1))h^{3(2\alpha-3)-\alpha+1}+32h^{\alpha-2}+(32+o(1))h^{3\alpha-5}+8h^{-\alpha}+32h^{\alpha-3}+24h^{3\alpha-6}-8<0,

Which means

(1+o(1))h^{5\alpha-8}+8h^{\alpha-2}+8h^{3\alpha-5}+2h^{-\alpha}+8h^{\alpha-3}+6h^{3\alpha-6}-2<0.

\square

D.3
Bounded Loss Gap for Approximate two-step loss for two-layer NN under orthogonal initialization

Here we consider the orthogonal initialization which make the setting
{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}=h{\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}{\bm{M}}={\bm{M}}{\bm{M}}^{{\top}}=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
.

We define that

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}

\displaystyle\;{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1\top}}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\;{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1\top}}

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\;{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1\top}}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle\;{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1\top}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}.

And we denote that

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0},

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0},

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}},

\displaystyle\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}},

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0},

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0},

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}},

\displaystyle\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}},

Lemma D.4
.

Under Assumption
3.3
and
3.2
, for
\eta_{1}
,
\eta_{2}\leq O(h\sqrt{h})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}})\right|\leq O(\frac{1}{h}).

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}})\right|\leq O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}).

Proof of Lemma
5.2
.

We first give bounded loss gap for approximate one-step loss under orthogonal initialization for two-layer NN. For one step, we consider
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\leq O(h^{\frac{3}{2}}).

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(86)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\right)\right|

Consider similar techniques in Lemma
C.6
, we get that

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}},

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{A}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}},

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\leq\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}},

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{A}}^{0}_{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}},

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{2}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{B}}^{0}_{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}},

taking these inequalities into (
117
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(87)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}\sqrt{h}}\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}})

Also,

\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}+\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|\leq 2\sqrt{\max(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}},\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}},\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}},1)}.

(88)

We combine (
87
), (
88
) and Assumption
E.1
, finally we get that

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}\sqrt{\max(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}},\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}},\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}},1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq O(\frac{1}{h})

\square

We are here considering the 2-step loss under orthogonal initialization for two-layer NN.
We have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(89)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

We first give bounded loss gap for
\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|
. Similar to (
86
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(90)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert-\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{2}}-\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

We know that

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}}\right){\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\left(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{6}}\right){\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}+\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}}\right){\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\left(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h^{6}}\right){{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{3}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}

We consider
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\leq O(h^{\frac{3}{2}})
, it is easy to find that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq O(1)+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h\sqrt{h}}=O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq O(1)+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}=O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}).

Combining (
90
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}^{1}_{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}\sqrt{h}}\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}})

Follow the same way to (
88
), we can obtain that

\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}+\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|\leq O(1).

Finally we get that

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}\right)\leq O(\frac{1}{h})

We now give bounded loss gap for
\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|
.

We have

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1},

\displaystyle\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}},

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1},

\displaystyle\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}},

For
{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

(91)

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1},

For
\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

(92)

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

Based on (
91
) and (
92
), we have

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}-\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\eta_{1}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}({\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}){\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}({\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}){\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}({\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}){\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}({\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}){\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}({\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}})+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}({\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}})-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}({\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}})+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}({\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}-\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}})+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

We know that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}}),\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}}),\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}),\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(1)+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h\sqrt{h}}=O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(1)+\frac{\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}=O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\eta_{1}\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert+\eta_{1}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\eta_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}}),\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}}),\left\lVert{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{h}\left\lVert{\bm{M}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{1}{h\sqrt{h}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}),\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}),\left\lVert{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert\leq\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}\right\rVert^{2}\leq O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}).

We find that

\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}-\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert\leq 3\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}+2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h\sqrt{h}}+12\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}}+6\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+5\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}+3\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}})

(93)

Similar to (
90
)

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(94)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}-\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}})

Follow the same way to (
88
), we can obtain that

\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}+\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|\leq O(1).

Finally we get that

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}\right)\leq O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}})

Thus, due to (
89
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(95)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle+
