\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\overline{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}})+O(\frac{1}{h})=O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}).

\square

D.4
Approximate one-step loss under orthogonal initialization for three-layer NN

Theorem D.5
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
3.2
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h})
based on Proposition
C.4
and
C.11
, consider the training procedure discussed in Section
3
, we derive the test loss after one-step and two-step GD update in a three-layer neural network:

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}

(96)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h}-\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+1

\displaystyle L_{\text{three-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})}{h^{2}}-1\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{10\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{37\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{12\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{5}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{4}\eta_{2}^{4}}{h^{6}}

We prove Theorem
5.5
above by the following two subsection
D.4
and
D.5
.

For orthogonal initialization we assume
n=h=d
.

Here we consider the orthogonal initialization where we make the setting
{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}=h{\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}=1,\mathbb{E}[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}},{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{{\top}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}=1,\mathbb{E}[{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{{\top}}]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
.

We consider a test data
\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}
under three-layer setting, where
\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}
is an random orthogonal vector, we have

\displaystyle L({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},{\bm{a}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

(97)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)^{\top}{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}-{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right)+tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right).

Here we define
L_{1},L_{2},L_{3},L_{4}
, where

\displaystyle L_{1}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{2}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{3}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{4}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right)

Thus

L_{\text{three-layer}}=L_{1}-L_{2}-L_{3}+L_{4}

We have
L_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{16}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}\right]\right)=\frac{1}{h},

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}},

\displaystyle T_{5}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{8}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{9}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{10}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}},

\displaystyle T_{12}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{13}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}},

\displaystyle T_{14}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{15}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{16}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}.

We have
L_{2}=\sum_{i=17}^{20}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{17}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{18}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h},

\displaystyle T_{19}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}}{h},

\displaystyle T_{20}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)=0.

We have
L_{3}=\sum_{i=21}^{24}T_{i}
, where

\displaystyle T_{21}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right]\right)=0,

\displaystyle T_{22}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h},

\displaystyle T_{23}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right]\right)=\frac{\eta_{2}}{h},

\displaystyle T_{24}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right]\right)=0,

Based on the above computation, we see that for orthogonal initialization, the one-step test loss for 3-layer NN is

L_{\text{three-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},{\bm{a}}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h}-\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+1

(98)

Here, for the one-step updated loss, we consider the following optimization problem, and we assume the following constraint
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, our goal is to see whether
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is local minima or local maxima.

L_{\text{three-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},{\bm{a}}^{1},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}-4h^{\alpha-1}+\frac{1}{h}+1+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})

(99)

It is easy to find that
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is a local maxima.
\square

D.5
Approximate two-step loss for three-layer NN under orthogonal initialization

For orthogonal initialization we assume
n=h=d
.

Here we consider the orthogonal initialization where we make the setting
{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}=h{\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}={\bm{I}},{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}=1,\mathbb{E}[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}},{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{{\top}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}=1,\mathbb{E}[{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{{\top}}]=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{I}}
.

For the simplification, we only consider replacing
{\bm{G}}_{1}
with
{\bm{A}}_{1}
and
{\bm{G}}_{2}
with
{\bm{A}}_{2}
. We consider
s=\frac{1}{h}

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\;\widetilde{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

Thus we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}+\eta_{1}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{1}}=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}+\frac{\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\top}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{1}}=\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}+\frac{\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{2\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

we can derive that

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+(\frac{2\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}){\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}^{\top}

\displaystyle={{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}+(\frac{2\eta_{1}}{\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}}{\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}}{h\sqrt{h}}){\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}

Thus, we have

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{21\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4((\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})^{2}(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})^{2})}{h^{4}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{5}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{4}\eta_{2}^{4}}{h^{6}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{4}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{4}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0\top}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{10\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{37\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4((\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})^{2}(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{12\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{5}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{4}\eta_{2}^{4}}{h^{6}}

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}^{\top}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})}{h^{2}}

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}}\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}}{\bm{a}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})}{h^{2}}

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{X}}}\left[{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}{{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=1

Thus, we have

L_{\text{three-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},{\bm{a}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})=\left(\frac{2(\eta_{1}+\eta_{2})(h+\eta_{1}\eta_{2})}{h^{2}}-1\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{10\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{37\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{12\eta_{1}^{3}\eta_{2}^{3}}{h^{5}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{4}\eta_{2}^{4}}{h^{6}}

(100)

\square

Corollary D.6
.

Suppose
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
and we consider
0<\alpha<1
.
Then, for any
\alpha
in this range, the point
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is not a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{three-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})
.
Moreover, for
0<\alpha\leq\tfrac{2}{3}
, if
h>h^{*}
, then
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{three-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2}})
, where
{h^{*}}
is the root of the following equation:

\displaystyle 32h^{3\alpha-2}+33h^{\alpha-1}+74h^{\alpha-2}+2h^{-\alpha}

(101)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 10h^{-\alpha-1}+36h^{3\alpha-3}+4h^{5\alpha-4}-8=0

Proof of Corollary
D.6
.

Here, for the two-step updated loss, we consider the following optimization problem, and we assume that
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, we want to find whether the local minima for
L_{\text{three-layer}}
is
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
.

Since
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{\text{three-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},{\bm{a}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{4(h+\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1}))}{h^{2-\alpha}}-1\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{2}}+\frac{10\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{2}}{h^{3}}+\frac{37\eta_{1}^{2}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{12\eta_{1}^{3}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{3}}{h^{5}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{4}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{4}}{h^{6}}

Taking the derivative, we have

\displaystyle L^{\prime}_{\text{three-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{2},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{2},{\bm{a}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=2(h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})\left[\frac{8}{h^{2-\alpha}}\left(\frac{4(\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})+h)}{h^{2-\alpha}}-1\right)\right]

\displaystyle+2(h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})\left[\frac{2}{h^{2}}+\frac{10}{h^{3}}+\frac{(h+74)\eta_{1}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})}{h^{4}}+\frac{36\eta_{1}^{2}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{2}}{h^{5}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{3}(2h^{\alpha}-\eta_{1})^{3}}{h^{6}}\right]

If we let
L_{\text{two-layer}}
is
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
be local minima, we must need

•

2>2-\alpha\Rightarrow\alpha>0

•

2\alpha-3<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<1

•

2\alpha-4<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<2

•

4\alpha-5<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<1

•

6\alpha-6<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<\frac{4}{5}

•

2\alpha-3<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<1

•

4\alpha-4<\alpha-2\Rightarrow\alpha<\frac{2}{3}

Take the intersection, we have
0<\alpha<\frac{2}{3}
. Given the fixed
0<\alpha<\frac{2}{3}
, we will give how large
h
is to ensure that
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
will be the local minima,

We need

32h^{3\alpha-2}+33h^{\alpha-1}+74h^{\alpha-2}+2h^{-\alpha}+10h^{-\alpha-1}+36h^{3\alpha-3}+4h^{5\alpha-4}-8<0.

\square

Appendix E
Gaussian Initialization

In this section, to obtain more general and practical results, we extend the one-step loss analysis to gaussian initialization while also accounting for label noise.

Assumption E.1
.

For gaussian initialization, we consider more general case with
d=h
, we also assume
c_{1}n\leq h\leq C_{1}n
, where
C_{1},c_{1}
are finite constants.

Dataset.

Here we use linear
teacher
models to generate the training data of both two-layer and three-layer
student
networks under gaussian initialization.
We sample
n
data points
\{{\bm{x}}_{1},\cdots,{\bm{x}}_{n}\}
from the isotropic Gaussian
{\bm{x}}_{i}\sim{\mathcal{N}}({\mathbf{0}}_{h},{\bm{I}}_{h}),\forall i\in[n]
as our input data.

•

Two-layer NN Case.
For a given
{\bm{x}}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h}
, we use a linear
teacher
model
F:{\mathbb{R}}^{h}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{h}
to generate the corresponding label
{\bm{y}}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h}

(
5
)
as follows

{\bm{y}}_{i}=F({\bm{x}}_{i})+{\bm{\xi}}^{\prime}_{i}={\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{x}}_{i}+{\bm{\xi}}^{\prime}_{i}.

(102)

Here,
{\bm{M}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h\times h}
with entries sampled i.i.d as follows
h\left[{\bm{M}}\right]_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
is the
target matrix
, and
{\bm{\xi}}^{\prime}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h}
with entries sampled i.i.d as follows
\sqrt{h}\left[{\bm{\xi}}^{\prime}_{i}\right]_{j}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,\rho_{e}^{2})
is the independent additive label noise. We represent
{\bm{X}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times h},{\bm{Y}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times h}

as the input matrix and the label matrix, respectively.

•

Three-layer NN Case.
For a given
{\bm{x}}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h}
, we use a linear teacher model
F^{*}:{\mathbb{R}}^{h}\to{\mathbb{R}}
to generate the corresponding scalar label
y_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}
as follows:

y_{i}=F^{*}({\bm{x}}_{i})+\xi_{i}={\bm{\beta}}^{*\top}{\bm{x}}_{i}+{\bm{\xi}}_{i}.

(103)

Here
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{h}
with
\sqrt{h}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
is the
target direction
, and
{\bm{\xi}}_{i}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,\rho_{e}^{2})
is the independent additive label noise.
We represent
{\bm{X}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times h},{\bm{y}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
as the input matrix and the label vector, respectively.

Model.

For two-layer and three-layer NNs, we consider the entries sampled i.i.d follows
\sqrt{h}\left[{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right]_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
,
\sqrt{h}\left[{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]_{i,j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
,
\sqrt{h}\left[{\bm{a}}\right]_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1),\forall i\in[h],j\in[h]
.

E.1
Norm Analysis of One-step Update Gradient Matrices Under Gaussian Initialization

We first give the norm analysis of one-step update gradient matrices under gaussian initialization. This analysis is an important step in simplifying the derivation of the theoretical test loss in the next Section (Section
E.2
). It also provides intuition about the range of learning rates that are beneficial for model training and offers a deeper understanding of the gradient matrices. Here, we follow the work of
3
and examine the norm properties of the hidden layers’ gradient matrices during a one-step update under both the three-layer and two-layer NN settings.
We give the norm analysis of three-layer NN setting as an example, for the norm analysis of two-layer NN setting, please see Appendix
C.1.4
.

The one-step update equations for the three-layer NN are as follows:

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}-\eta_{1}{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0},\hskip 10.0pt{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{2}{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0},

(104)

where
{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}
are the initial hidden layer weights,
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are the learning rate for the first layer and second layer, respectively.
{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}
and
{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}
are the updated layer weights.
{\bm{G}}_{1}
and
{\bm{G}}_{2}
are the corresponding gradient matrices, where

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}}_{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{n\sqrt{h}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}}_{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}},

(105)

\displaystyle{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}}_{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{n\sqrt{h}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}}_{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}.

(106)

By analyzing the norm of
{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}
, we have the following proposition:

Proposition E.2
.

(Three-layer NN setting under gaussian initialization.) Under Assumption
E.1
, there exists some constant
c^{*}>0
such that for all large
n,h
with probability at least
1-32e^{-c^{*}n}-30n^{4}e^{-c^{*}\sqrt{n}}
, we have gradient approximation,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert,

(107)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}-{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}-1}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert.

We obtain the norm control of gradient matrices,

\displaystyle\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\Theta_{h,\mathbb{P}}(1),\hskip 10.0pt\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{h,\mathbb{P}}(1),

(108)

\displaystyle\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\Theta_{h,\mathbb{P}}(1),\hskip 10.0pt\sqrt{h}\left\lVert{\bm{G}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}=\Theta_{h,\mathbb{P}}(1).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle Small\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert

(109)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(\sqrt{h})\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert

(110)

\displaystyle Large\ lr:\eta_{1}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

(111)

\displaystyle\eta_{2}=\Theta(h)\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}\asymp\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert_{F}

(112)

We provide the complete proof in the Appendix
C.1.3
. A similar result can be obtained for the two-layer NN setting. See Proposition
C.9
, we provide the proof in Appendix
C.1.4
.

Proposition
E.2
shows that in terms of norm,
\{{\bm{A}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
is very close to
\{{\bm{G}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
, which means
\{{\bm{A}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
serves as the leading term in
\{{\bm{G}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
. This approximation can significantly simplify the subsequent analysis of the theoretical test loss for the three-layer NN when we replace the gradients
\{{\bm{G}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
with their approximated version
\{{\bm{A}}_{i}^{0}\}_{i=1}^{2}
.
For the two-layer NN setting, we similarly replace the original gradient with its leading term, as justified by Proposition
C.9
and Lemma
E.3
, to simplify the test loss analysis.

E.2
Relationship between Test Loss and Layer-wise Learning Rates

In this section, we first derive the theoretical test loss after a one-step update for both two-layer and three-layer neural networks under our setup. Based on this theoretical test loss, we vary the learning rates for each layer in these two networks.
We aim to determine whether using the same learning rates across layers leads to minimal test loss for networks trained with a one-step GD update when
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
, where
h^{\alpha}\leq

Large lr
.

E.2.1
Two-layer Neural Networks

Given test data
\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\!\sim\!{\mathcal{N}}({\mathbf{0}},{\bm{I}}_{h})
, we consider the test loss

L_{\text{two-layer}}=\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}}^{\prime},{\bm{M}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\|^{2}.

The key lemma in this subsection uses the approximate gradient to replace the true gradient updates, thereby simplifying the analysis of the test loss.

Lemma E.3
.

We define the following matrices:

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}},{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}},

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}},{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}.

We also define
{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\prime}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}^{\prime}
and
{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\prime}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}^{\prime}
.
Then, under Assumption
3.3
and
E.1
, for
\eta_{1}
,
\eta_{2}
no more than
O(h\sqrt{h})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\prime},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\prime})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\prime},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\prime})\right|\leq O(\frac{1}{h}).

The simplified analysis leads to the following result for two-layer networks.

Theorem E.4
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
E.1
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h\sqrt{h}})
, based on Proposition
C.9
and Lemma
E.3
, consider the training procedure discussed in Section
3
, we obtain the following test loss after one-step GD update in a two-layer neural network under gaussian initialization:

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}=\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}

(113)

\displaystyle-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}(\rho_{e}^{2}+1)^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{4}}+1+\frac{1}{h}

\displaystyle+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{5}}{h^{2}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}).

The complete proof is provided in Appendix
E.3
.

Analysis of Special Cases.

Here, we consider a special case. Specifically, we take
h=n=d
and
\rho_{e}=0
, under which the loss simplifies to:

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}=\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{4\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}

(114)

\displaystyle+1+\frac{1}{h}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{5}}{h^{2}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}).

Taking special case (
114
) as an example
114
, we obtain the following corollary for two-layer neural network under gaussian initialization.

Corollary E.5
.

Suppose
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
and we consider
0<\alpha\leq\tfrac{3}{2}
.
Then, for any
\alpha
in this range, the point
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is not a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{two-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}^{\prime},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}^{\prime})
.

We do simulations in Figure
6
in Appendix
F
to support Corollary
E.5
.

E.2.2
Three-layer Neural Networks

Given test data
\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\!\sim\!{\mathcal{N}}({\mathbf{0}},{\bm{I}}_{d})
, we consider the test loss

L_{\text{three-layer}}=\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{h}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}{\bm{a}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)^{2}

Theorem E.6
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
E.1
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h})
based on Proposition
E.2
, consider the training procedure discussed in Section
3
, we derive the test loss after one-step GD update in a three-layer neural network:

\displaystyle L_{\text{three-layer}}=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{hn}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}

(115)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}+1

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}}\right).

The complete proof is provided in Appendix
E.4
.

Analysis of Special Cases.

Here we consider a special case. Specifically, we take
h=n=d
and
\rho_{e}=0
, under which the loss becomes:

\displaystyle L_{\text{three-layer}}=\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{h}+\frac{4\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}-\frac{2\eta_{2}}{h}+\frac{4\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}h}{n^{2}h^{3}}

(116)

\displaystyle+1+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}\right)+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}}\right).

Taking special case (
116
) as an example, we obtain the following corollary for three-layer neural network under gaussian initialization.

Corollary E.7
.

Suppose
\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}=2h^{\alpha}
and we consider
0<\alpha<1
.
Then, for any
\alpha
in this range, the point
\eta_{1}=\eta_{2}=h^{\alpha}
is not a local minimum of the loss
L_{\text{three-layer}}({{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}})
.

We do simulations in Figure
8
in Appendix
F
to support Corollary
E.7
.

E.3
Two-layer NN Test Loss under Gaussian initialization

Lemma E.8
.

Consider that

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}

\displaystyle{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}

\displaystyle{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},

under Assumption
3.3
,
E.1
, consider
{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}
and
{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}
, then for
\eta_{1}
,
\eta_{2}\sim O(h\sqrt{h})
, we have

\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})\right|\leq O(\frac{1}{h})

Proof of Lemma
E.8
.

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(117)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert_{F}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{F}\left\lVert{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left(\left\lVert-\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

Consider similar techniques in Lemma
C.6
, we get that

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}),

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}}),

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{2}\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}),

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}\sqrt{h}}),

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}\right\rVert\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}),

taking these inequalities into (
117
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}-\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|

(118)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{h}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\left\lVert\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert+\left\lVert\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}\right\rVert\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}})

Also, based on the Assumption
3.3
and theorem
E.9
, we have

\left|\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}+\sqrt{L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})}\right|\leq O(1).

(119)

We combine (
118
), (
119
) and Assumption
E.1
, and assume
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}\sim O(h\sqrt{h})
, finally we get that

\left|L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})-L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})\right|\leq O(\frac{\eta_{1}+\eta_{2}}{h^{2}\sqrt{h}})\leq O(\frac{1}{h})

\square

Theorem E.9
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
E.1
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h\sqrt{h}})
based on Proposition
C.9
, consider training procedure discussed in section
3
, we derive the test loss after one-step GD update in a two-layer neural network under guassian initialization:

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}

(120)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}(\rho_{e}^{2}+1)^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{4}}

\displaystyle+1+\frac{1}{h}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{5}}{h^{2}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

Proof of Theorem
E.4
.

We consider a test data
\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\sim{\mathcal{N}}({\mathbf{0}},{\bm{I}}_{d})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1\times d}
under two-layer setting , we have

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}({\bm{X}},{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

(121)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left\lVert\frac{1}{h}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}{\bm{M}}\right\rVert_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}}^{\top}\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0}\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}-{\bm{M}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{h}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}-{\bm{M}}\right)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right).

Here we define
L_{1},L_{2},L_{3},L_{4}
, where

\displaystyle L_{1}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h^{2}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{2}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{\bm{M}}{{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\top}{{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{3}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{1}{h}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle L_{4}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{M}}}{{\bm{M}}}^{\top}\right]\right)

Thus

L_{\text{two-layer}}=L_{1}-L_{2}-L_{3}+L_{4}

Consider the exact gradient update,

\displaystyle{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}+\eta_{1}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}-\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}

\displaystyle{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}-\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}.

We have

\displaystyle{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}=

\displaystyle{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{1}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}-\eta_{1}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}-\eta_{2}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}-\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}},

where

\displaystyle{{\bm{A}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{3}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{A}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{3}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}

\displaystyle{{\bm{B}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{B}}_{2}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{4}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}.

Based on Lemma
E.8
, we consider replacing
{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}},{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}
with
{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}},{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}
.

Thus we have

\displaystyle{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}\approx{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1}}}{\widetilde{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1}}}

\displaystyle={\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}+\frac{\eta_{1}}{nh}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{2}}{nh}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{Y}}

.

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{4}}\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}\sum_{t=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}

\displaystyle{{\displaystyle\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{ki}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{pt}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{kt}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{pi}

For
Term_{1}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{4}}\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}\sum_{t=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}

{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{ki}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{X}}}_{pq}{{\bm{M}}}_{qt}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{kt}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{X}}}_{pq}{{\bm{M}}}_{qi}
, we consider the following cases:

Case 1.
k=k,i=t,m=m,q=q,p=p,m\neq q
.

Term_{1}^{1}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}\times h\times h\times(d^{2}-d)\times n\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{h}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{4}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{5}}).

Case 2.
k=k,i=t,m=m,q=q,p\neq p,m=q
.

Term_{1}^{2}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}\times h\times h\times d\times(n^{2}-n)\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{h}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{5}}).

Case 3.
k=k,i=t,m=m,q=q,p=p,m=q
.

Term_{1}^{3}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}\times h\times h\times d\times n\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{h}\times 3=O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{5}}).

Thus we have

Term_{1}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{4}}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{5}})

For
Term_{2}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{4}}\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{h}\sum_{k=1}^{h}\sum_{m=1}^{d}\sum_{p=1}^{n}

{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{ki}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{pt}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{kt}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{mk}{{\bm{X}}}_{pm}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{pi}
, we consider the following case:

Case 1.
k=k,i=t,m=m,p=p
.

Term_{2}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}\times h\times h\times d\times n\times\frac{1}{d}\times\frac{1}{h}\times\rho_{e}^{2}=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{4}}.

Combine
Term_{1}
and
Term_{2}

We finally get that

T_{4}=O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}})

(124)

\displaystyle{{\bm{\xi}}_{ki}}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{kq}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{qs}^{2}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{si}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{kc}{\bm{X}}_{pc}{\bm{X}}_{pk}{\bm{M}}_{ki}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{\bm{X}}_{mk}

We focus only on the case dominated by the leading term.

q=c=k
,

\displaystyle{\bm{X}}_{mi}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{qs}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{st}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{bt}{\bm{X}}_{bc}{\bm{X}}_{bc}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{bt}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{st}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{qs}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{\bm{X}}_{mi}

We focus only on the case dominated by the leading term.

Case 1.
q=i,m\neq m
.

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{5}d}\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}\sum_{s=1}^{h}\sum_{t=1}^{h}\sum_{b=1}^{d}\sum_{c=1}^{d}\ \sum_{m=1}^{n}{\bm{X}}_{mi}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{qs}^{2}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{st}^{2}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{bt}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{bc}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{\bm{X}}_{mi}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{6}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}}).

Case 2.
m=m,q\neq i
.

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{5}d}\mathbb{E}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{q=1}^{d}\sum_{s=1}^{h}\sum_{t=1}^{h}\sum_{b=1}^{d}\sum_{c=1}^{d}\ \sum_{m=1}^{n}{\bm{X}}_{mi}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}_{qs}^{2}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}_{st}^{2}{{\bm{\xi}}}_{bt}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{bc}^{2}{\bm{X}}_{mq}{\bm{X}}_{mi}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{6}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

It is easy to see

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{6}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{{\bm{\xi}}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{{\bm{\xi}}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{M}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{6}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{\xi}}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{M}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{{\bm{\xi}}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{6}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{6}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

Finally we get

T_{16}=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d}{n^{2}h^{6}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{dh^{6}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d\rho_{e}^{4}}{n^{2}h^{6}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{6}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{6}}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

(131)

\square

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}}{nh^{2}}{\bm{M}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{Y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}.

(133)

\displaystyle T_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}

(134)

\displaystyle T_{23}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}}{dh}.

(135)

\square

Finally, we obtain the exact loss

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}d(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{4}}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}

(136)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{dh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{ndh^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{dh}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d}{n^{2}h^{6}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{dh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d(\rho_{e}^{2}+1)^{2}}{n^{2}h^{6}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{dh^{6}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{6}}

\displaystyle+1+\frac{1}{h}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{5}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

Under Assumption
E.1
, we have

\displaystyle L_{\text{two-layer}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h^{2}}

(137)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh^{3}}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{4}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}(\rho_{e}^{2}+1)^{2}}{n^{2}h^{5}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{7}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{3}h^{4}}

\displaystyle+1+\frac{1}{h}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{5}}{h^{2}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{5}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{8}})

\square

E.4
Three-layer NN Test Loss under Gaussian initialization

Theorem E.10
.

Given Assumption
3.3
,
E.1
, and in addition assume
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
are no more than
O({h})
based on Proposition

, we derive the test loss after one-step GD update in a three-layer neural network:

\displaystyle L_{\text{three-layer}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{hn}-2\frac{\eta_{1}}{h}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}-2\frac{\eta_{2}}{h}

(138)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{1}\eta_{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}\rho_{e}^{2}}{nh^{3}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}d\rho_{e}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{3}}+\frac{4\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}

\displaystyle+1+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{3}})+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}})+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}})+O\left(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{5}}\right)

.

, we consider a test data

\displaystyle L({\bm{X}},{\bm{W}}_{1}^{1},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{1},{\bm{a}},\tilde{{\bm{x}}}_{0})

\displaystyle=

, we have

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]

let
{\bm{S}}_{2}={{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}
. By replacing them into
T_{6}
,

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right].

Here we first analyze
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i,j}a_{i}S_{2_{ij}}a_{j}\right)\left(\sum_{p,q}a_{p}S_{2_{pq}}a_{q}\right)\right]
, This can be reduced to the following three cases, since the expectations in all other cases are zero.

Case 1
(i,j)=(p,q),i\neq j
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}a_{i}S_{2_{ij}}a_{j}\right)^{2}\right]

(149)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,j\\
i\neq j\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}a_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}S_{2_{ij}}^{2}\mathbb{E}a_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=(h^{2}-h)\times\frac{1}{h}\times\frac{1}{h}\times\frac{1}{h}\times h\times\frac{1}{h}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}-\frac{1}{h^{2}}

Case 2
(i,j)=(q,p),i\neq j
.
Same to Case 1,

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]_{2}=\frac{1}{h}-\frac{1}{h^{2}}

(150)

Case 3
i=j,p=q
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]_{3}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i}S_{2_{ii}}a_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{p}S_{2_{pp}}a_{p}^{2}\right)\right]

(151)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,p}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(S_{2_{ii}}S_{2_{pp}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(a_{i}^{2}a_{p}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=p}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(S_{2_{ii}}^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(a_{i}^{4}\right)\right]+\sum_{i\neq p}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(S_{2_{ii}}S_{2_{pp}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(a_{i}^{2}a_{p}^{2}\right)\right].

For
\mathbb{E}S_{2_{ii}}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}S_{2_{ii}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}S_{2_{11}}^{2}

(152)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(W_{2_{11}}^{2}+W_{2_{21}}^{2}+\cdots+W_{2_{h1}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=h\times\frac{3}{h^{2}}+(h^{2}-h)\times\frac{1}{h}\times\frac{1}{h}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{2}{h}.

It is east to see

\mathbb{E}a_{i}^{4}=\frac{3}{h^{2}}

(153)

\mathbb{E}_{i\neq p}\left(S_{2_{ii}}S_{2_{pp}}\right)=\mathbb{E}S_{2_{ii}}\mathbb{E}S_{2_{pp}}=1

(154)

\mathbb{E}_{i\neq p}\left(a_{ii}^{2}a_{pp}^{2}\right)=\mathbb{E}a_{{ii}}^{2}\mathbb{E}a_{{pp}}^{2}=\frac{1}{h^{2}}

(155)

by combining (
152
) to (
155
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]_{3}

\displaystyle=h\times(1+\frac{1}{h^{2}})\times\frac{3}{h^{2}}+(h^{2}-h)\times\frac{1}{h^{2}}

(156)

\displaystyle=1+\frac{2}{h}+\frac{6}{h^{2}}.

By combining (
149
),(
150
)and(
156
), we have

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{S}}_{2}{\bm{a}}\right]=1+\frac{4}{h}+\frac{4}{h^{2}}.

(157)

\square

We then analyze
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]
. note that
{\bm{y}}={\bm{X}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}+{\bm{\xi}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+{\bm{\xi}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}+{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right)\right]

(158)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\xi}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{\xi}}\right)

\displaystyle=tr\left[\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right)\right]+tr\left[\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}\right)\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\xi}}{\bm{\xi}}^{\top}\right)\right].

For
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)
, we show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)^{2}_{ii}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)^{2}_{11}

(159)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}X^{2}_{m1}\right)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=2}^{d}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}X_{k1}X_{kj}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=3n+(n^{2}-n)+(d-1)n

\displaystyle=n^{2}+nd+n

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)^{2}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}X_{m1}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}X_{k1}X_{k2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}X_{m2}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}X_{k2}X_{k1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}X_{k1}X_{kj}\right)\left(\sum_{p=1}^{n}X_{pj}X_{p2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=0.

Which means
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)=(n^{2}+nd+n){\bm{I}}_{d}
.

It is easy to see
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\beta}}^{*}{\bm{\beta}}^{*^{\top}}\right)=\frac{1}{d}{\bm{I}}_{d}
,
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}\right)=n{\bm{I}}_{n}
,
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{\xi}}{\bm{\xi}}^{\top}\right)=\rho_{e}^{2}{\bm{I}}_{n}
. Thus, consider (
158
) we have

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]=n^{2}+nd(1+\rho_{e}^{2})+n.

(160)

\square

By (
157
) and (
160
), we finally get

\displaystyle T_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\left(n^{2}+nd(1+\rho_{e}^{2})+n\right)\left(1+\frac{4}{h}+\frac{4}{h^{4}}\right)}{n^{2}h^{2}}

(161)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}d(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{h^{2}n}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}}{h^{3}})

\square

For
T_{11}
, similar to
T_{6}
, let
{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}={\bm{S}}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right)\right)

For
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{h}a_{i}^{2}\right)^{2}

(162)

\displaystyle=h\mathbb{E}\left(a_{1}^{4}\right)+(h^{2}-h)\mathbb{E}\left(a_{1}^{2}a_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=h\times\frac{3}{h^{2}}+(h^{2}-h)\times\frac{1}{h}\times\frac{1}{h}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{2}{h}

Take (
162
) into
T_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{h}\right)tr\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{2}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right)\right]

(163)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{h}\right)tr\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{S}}_{1}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{h}\right)tr\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{S}}_{1}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle.

Similar to compute
\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left({\bm{S}}_{1}^{2}\right)=\frac{h^{2}+h+hd}{d^{2}}{\bm{I}}_{d}.

(164)

By taking (

) into

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{h}\right)\frac{h^{2}+h+hd}{d^{2}}tr\mathbb{E}\left[\left({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right)\right].

), we have

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\left(1+\frac{2}{h}\right)\left(\frac{h^{2}+h+hd}{d^{2}}\right)\left(n^{2}+nd(1+\rho_{e}^{2})+n\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{hd}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}+\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nd}+O(\frac{\eta_{2}^{2}}{h^{3}}).

\square

and

\displaystyle=tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\mathbb{E}tr\left(\left[{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}h^{2}}\mathbb{E}tr\left(\left[{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}hd}\mathbb{E}tr\left(\left[{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}hd}\mathbb{E}tr\left(\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{n^{2}hd}\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}\right].

) and (

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{hd}+\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}(1+\rho_{e}^{2})}{nh}+O(\frac{\eta_{1}\eta_{2}}{h^{3}}).

, so we have

\square

Analysis of
T_{16}
.

\displaystyle T_{16}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0},{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0},{\bm{a}},{\bm{\xi}},{\bm{X}}}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta_{1}^{2}\eta_{2}^{2}}{n^{4}h^{3}}tr\left(\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}^{\top}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{y}}{\bm{y}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}{{\bm{W}}_{1}^{0}}{{\bm{W}}_{2}^{0}}\right]\right)

For
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]_{11}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{h}a_{i}^{2}\right)^{2}a_{1}^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{h}+O(\frac{1}{h^{2}}),

(171)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}{\bm{a}}{\bm{a}}^{\top}\right]_{11}
